<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;section=2" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<ul class="etymonid" data-lang="bg" id="Bulgarian:&#95;book" data-id="book" data-title="книга"></ul>
<p><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#inherited" title="Appendix:Glossary">Inherited</a> from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Slavic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Slavic language">Proto-Slavic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="sla-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Slavic/k%D1%8A%C5%88iga" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Slavic/kъňiga">&#42;kъňiga</a></i>.
</p>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;section=4" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Cyrl headword" lang="bg">кни́га</strong> <a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:Bulgarian_transliteration" title="Wiktionary:Bulgarian transliteration">•</a> (<span lang="bg-Latn" class="headword-tr tr Latn" dir="ltr">kníga</span>)&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="feminine gender">f</abbr></span> (<i>relational adjective</i> <b class="Cyrl form-of lang-bg relational&#95;adjective-form-of origin-кни́га" lang="bg"><a href="/wiki/%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B6%D0%B5%D0%BD#Bulgarian" title="книжен">кни́жен</a></b>, <i>diminutive</i> <b class="Cyrl form-of lang-bg diminutive-form-of origin-кни́га" lang="bg"><a href="/wiki/%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B6%D0%BA%D0%B0#Bulgarian" title="книжка">кни́жка</a></b> <i>or</i> <b class="Cyrl form-of lang-bg diminutive-form-of origin-кни́га" lang="bg"><a href="/wiki/%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B6%D0%BB%D0%B5#Bulgarian" title="книжле">книжле́</a></b>)</span>
</p>

<dl><dd><div class="h-usage-example"><i class="Cyrl mention e-example" lang="bg">Етаже́рката отсре́ща бе натъ́пкана с <b>кни́ги</b>.</i><dl><dd><i lang="bg-Latn" class="e-transliteration tr Latn">Etažérkata otsréšta be natǎ́pkana s <b>knígi</b>.</i></dd><dd><span class="e-translation">The shelf across was stuffed with <b>books</b>.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></li>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_4">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;section=17" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<div class="inflection-table-notes">
<div style="max-width: 24em"><sup>1</sup> Animate nouns almost exclusively take the type II accusative ending, whereas inanimate nouns can be used with either ending, but are more often found with type I.</div>

<th class="secondary">II
</th>
<td><span class="Cyrl" lang="kpv"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0%D1%82%D1%96%D1%8B%D0%BC&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="книгатіым (page does not exist)">книгатіым</a></span> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="kpv-Latn" class="tr Latn">kńigatiym</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>

</td></tr></tbody></table>
<div class="inflection-table-notes">
<div style="max-width: 24em"><sup>1</sup> Animate nouns almost exclusively take the type II accusative ending, whereas inanimate nouns can be used with either ending, but are more often found with type I.</div>

<th class="secondary">II
</th>
<td><span class="Cyrl" lang="kpv"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0%D1%82%D1%96%D1%8B%D0%B4&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="книгатіыд (page does not exist)">книгатіыд</a></span> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="kpv-Latn" class="tr Latn">kńigatiyd</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>

</td></tr></tbody></table>
<div class="inflection-table-notes">
<div style="max-width: 24em"><sup>1</sup> Animate nouns almost exclusively take the type II accusative ending, whereas inanimate nouns can be used with either ending, but are more often found with type I.</div>

<th class="secondary">II
</th>
<td><span class="Cyrl" lang="kpv"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0%D1%82%D1%96%D1%8B%D1%81&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="книгатіыс (page does not exist)">книгатіыс</a></span> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="kpv-Latn" class="tr Latn">kńigatiys</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>

</td></tr></tbody></table>
<div class="inflection-table-notes">
<div style="max-width: 24em"><sup>1</sup> Animate nouns almost exclusively take the type II accusative ending, whereas inanimate nouns can be used with either ending, but are more often found with type I.</div>

<th class="secondary">II
</th>
<td><span class="Cyrl" lang="kpv"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0%D1%82%D1%96%D0%BD%D1%8B%D0%BC&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="книгатіным (page does not exist)">книгатіным</a></span> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="kpv-Latn" class="tr Latn">kńigatinym</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>

</td></tr></tbody></table>
<div class="inflection-table-notes">
<div style="max-width: 24em"><sup>1</sup> Animate nouns almost exclusively take the type II accusative ending, whereas inanimate nouns can be used with either ending, but are more often found with type I.</div>

</th>

</td></tr></tbody></table>
<div class="inflection-table-notes">
<div style="max-width: 24em"><sup>1</sup> Animate nouns almost exclusively take the type II accusative ending, whereas inanimate nouns can be used with either ending, but are more often found with type I.</div>

<td><span class="Cyrl" lang="kpv"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0%D3%A7%D0%B4%D0%BD%D1%8B%D1%81&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="книгаӧдныс (page does not exist)">книгаӧдныс</a></span> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="kpv-Latn" class="tr Latn">kńigaödnys</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>
</td>
<td><span class="Cyrl" lang="kpv"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0%D1%8F%D1%81%D3%A7%D0%B4%D0%BD%D1%8B%D1%81&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="книгаясӧдныс (page does not exist)">книгаясӧдныс</a></span> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="kpv-Latn" class="tr Latn">kńigajasödnys</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>
</td></tr>
<tr>
<th class="secondary">II
</th>
<td><span class="Cyrl" lang="kpv"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0%D1%82%D1%96%D0%BD%D1%8B%D1%81&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="книгатіныс (page does not exist)">книгатіныс</a></span> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="kpv-Latn" class="tr Latn">kńigatinys</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>
</td>
<td><span class="Cyrl" lang="kpv"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0%D1%8F%D1%81%D1%82%D1%96%D0%BD%D1%8B%D1%81&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="книгаястіныс (page does not exist)">книгаястіныс</a></span> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="kpv-Latn" class="tr Latn">kńigajastinys</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>
</td></tr></tbody></table>
<div class="inflection-table-notes">
<div style="max-width: 24em"><sup>1</sup> Animate nouns almost exclusively take the type II accusative ending, whereas inanimate nouns can be used with either ending, but are more often found with type I.</div>
</div>
</div>
</td></tr></tbody></table>
</div>
</div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Old_East_Slavic">Old East Slavic</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;section=28" title="Edit section: Old East Slavic"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_6">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;section=30" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Cyrs headword" lang="orv">книга</strong> (<span lang="orv-Latn" class="headword-tr tr Latn" dir="ltr">kniga</span>)&#160;<span class="gender"><abbr title="feminine gender">f</abbr></span> (<i>diminutive</i> <b class="Cyrs" lang="orv"><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B6%D1%8C%D0%BA%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;redlink=1" class="new" title="книжька (page does not exist)">книжька</a></b>, <i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#relational" title="Appendix:Glossary">related adjective</a></i> <b class="Cyrs" lang="orv"><a href="/wiki/%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B6%D1%8C%D0%BD%D1%8A#Old_East_Slavic" title="книжьнъ">книжьнъ</a></b>)</span>
</p>
<ol><li><span class="form-of-definition use-with-mention">alternative form of <span class="form-of-definition-link"><i class="Cyrs mention" lang="orv"><a href="/wiki/%D0%BA%D1%8A%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0#Old_East_Slavic" title="кънига">кънига</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="orv-Latn" class="mention-tr tr Latn">kŭniga</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span></span></span>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1076</b></span>, <cite><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/ru:%D0%98%D0%B7%D0%B1%D0%BE%D1%80%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%BA_1076_%D0%B3%D0%BE%D0%B4%D0%B0" class="extiw" title="w:ru:Изборник 1076 года">Sviatoslav's izbornik</a></cite>&#8206;<sup><a rel="nofollow" class="external autonumber" href="https://expositions.nlr.ru/literature/drevrus/show_Manuscripts.php?i=F0557166-7483-4F3C-853F-74B3D5424DC5&amp;v=XI&amp;l=3">[1]</a></sup>, page 2:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Cyrs e-quotation cited-passage" lang="orv">ѥгда чьтеши <b>книгꙑ</b>· не тъштисѧ бърꙁо иштисти до дроугыꙗ главиꙁнꙑ·</span><dl><dd><i lang="orv-Latn" class="e-transliteration tr Latn">jegda čĭteši <b>knigy</b>· ne tŭštisę bŭrzo ištisti do drugyja glavizny·</i></dd><dd><span class="e-translation">when you read books, don't strive to quickly read on to the following chapter.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Noun_7">Noun</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;section=36" title="Edit section: Noun"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p><span class="headword-line"><strong class="Cyrl headword" lang="ru">кни́га</strong> <a href="/wiki/Wiktionary:Russian_transliteration" title="Wiktionary:Russian transliteration">•</a> (<span class="Cyrl" lang="ru"><a href="/wiki/%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B8#Russian" title="книги">кни́ги</a></b>, <i>nominative plural</i> <b class="Cyrl" lang="ru"><a href="/wiki/%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B8#Russian" title="книги">кни́ги</a></b>, <i>genitive plural</i> <b class="Cyrl" lang="ru"><a href="/wiki/%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3#Russian" title="книг">книг</a></b>, <i><a href="/wiki/Appendix:Glossary#relational_adjective" title="Appendix:Glossary">relational adjective</a></i> <b class="Cyrl" lang="ru"><a class="Cyrl form-of lang-ru diminutive-form-of target-кни́жка origin-кни́га origin&#95;transliteration-" lang="ru"><a href="/wiki/%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B6%D0%BA%D0%B0#Russian" title="книжка">кни́жка</a></b> <i>or</i> <b class="Cyrl form-of lang-ru diminutive-form-of target-кни́жечка origin-кни́га origin&#95;transliteration-" lang="ru"><a href="/wiki/%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B6%D0%B5%D1%87%D0%BA%D0%B0#Russian" title="книжечка">кни́жечка</a></b> <i>or</i> <b class="Cyrl form-of lang-ru diminutive-form-of target-кни́жица origin-кни́га </p>
<ol><li><a href="/wiki/book" title="book">book</a>
<dl><dd><span class="nyms holonym"><span style="font-size: smaller">Holonym:</span> <span class="Cyrl" lang="ru"><a href="/wiki/%D0%B1%D0%B8%D0%B1%D0%BB%D0%B8%D0%BE%D1%82%D0%B5%D0%BA%D0%B0#Russian" title="библиотека">библиоте́ка</a></span> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="ru-Latn" class="tr Latn">bibliotéka</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span></span></dd>
<dd><span class="h-usage-example"><i class="Cyrl mention e-example" lang="ru"><a href="/wiki/%D0%BE%D0%BD#Russian" title="он">Он</a> <a href="/wiki/%D0%BF%D1%80%D0%B5%D0%BF%D0%BE%D0%B4%D0%BD%D0%B5%D1%81%D1%82%D0%B8#Russian" title="преподнести">преподнёс</a> <a href="/wiki/%D0%BE%D0%BD%D0%B0#Russian" title="она">ей</a> <b>кни́гу</b>.</i> ― <i lang="ru-Latn" class="e-transliteration tr Latn">On prepodnjós jej <b>knígu</b>.</i> ― <span class="e-translation">He presented <b>a book</b> to her. / He presented her with <b>a book</b>.</span></span></dd></dl>
<ul><li><div class="citation-whole"><span class="cited-source"><span class="None" lang="und"><b>1937</b></span>, <a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Lion_Feuchtwanger" class="extiw" title="w:Lion Feuchtwanger">Lion Feuchtwanger</a>, <cite>Moscow 1937: My Visit Described for My Friends. 2. Conformism and Individualism</cite>, page <span class="None" lang="und">42</span>:</span><dl><dd><div class="h-quotation"><span class="Cyrl e-quotation cited-passage" lang="ru"><b>Кни́ги</b> представля́ют собо́й не́что це́нное, хотя́ и продаю́тся по весьма́ дешёвым це́нам, так что когда́ мне сказа́ли&#58; «де́ньги Вы мо́жете оставля́ть неза́пертыми, но <b>кни́ги</b> свои́ держи́те, пожа́луйста, под замко́м», то я отнёсся к э́тому не про́сто как к шу́тке.</span><dl><dd><i lang="ru-Latn" class="e-transliteration tr Latn"><b>Knígi</b> predstavljájut sobój néčto cénnoje, xotjá i prodajútsja po vesʹmá dešóvym cénam, tak što kogdá mne skazáli&#58; «dénʹgi Vy móžete ostavljátʹ nezápertymi, no <b>knígi</b> svoí deržíte, požálujsta, pod zamkóm», to ja otnjóssja k étomu ne prósto kak k šútke.</i></dd><dd><span class="e-translation">Although sold at very low prices, books are precious things, and I treated it as more than a joke when I was told that I could leave money lying about, but that my books should be kept under lock and key.</span></dd></dl></div></dd></dl></div></li></ul></li></ol>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Udmurt">Udmurt</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;section=44" title="Edit section: Udmurt"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_7">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;section=45" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>Borrowed from <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Russian_language" class="extiw" title="w:Russian language">Russian</a></span> <i class="Cyrl mention" lang="ru"><a class="mw-selflink-fragment" href="#Russian">книга</a></i> <span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">(</span><span lang="ru-Latn" class="mention-tr tr Latn">kniga</span><span class="mention-gloss-paren annotation-paren">)</span>.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Pronunciation_6">Pronunciation</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;section=46" title="Edit section: Pronunciation"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>

<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Ukrainian">Ukrainian</h2><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;section=51" title="Edit section: Ukrainian"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading3"><h3 id="Etymology_8">Etymology</h3><span class="mw-editsection"><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">[</span><a href="/w/index.php?title=%D0%BA%D0%BD%D0%B8%D0%B3%D0%B0&amp;action=edit&amp;section=52" title="Edit section: Etymology"><span>edit</span></a><span class="mw-editsection-bracket">]</span></span></div>
<p>From <span class="etyl"><a href="https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Proto-Slavic_language" class="extiw" title="w:Proto-Slavic language">Proto-Slavic</a></span> <i class="Latn mention" lang="sla-pro"><a href="/wiki/Reconstruction:Proto-Slavic/k%D1%8A%C5%88iga" title="Reconstruction:Proto-Slavic/kъňiga">&#42;kъňiga</a></i>.
</p>
